<section class="sections-normal-padding bg-master-lighter dotted-section" data-dot-id="section-2">
<div class="container">
<div class="row">
<div class="col-sm-12 text-center">
<p class="x6 x-red m-b-0">Our Services</p>

<div class="col-sm-12 text-center p-b-35">
<h2 class="m-t-0">The perfect solutions to protect and expand your business</h2>
</div>

<div class="carousel slide multiitem-car" id="services-carousel" style="margin-bottom:0">

<a class="btn-floating btn-xlg btn-primary" href="/en/trademarks">
<span class="halflings halflings-registration-mark"></span>
</a>
</div>
<h3 class="x3-strong x-red">Trademarks</h3>
<p class="m-b-30">Trademarks grant you the exclusive rights over the use phrases, images and logos that are associated with your business.</p>
</div>
<div class="col-sm-4 item-card hidden-xs">
<a class="btn-floating btn-xlg btn-primary" href="/en/patent">
<span class="glyphicons glyphicons-lightbulb"></span>
</a>
<h3 class="x3-strong x-red">Patents</h3>
<p class="m-b-30">Patents are a way to protect your inventions, granting you the exclusivity over their yield for a period of time.</p>

<script data-rvolt-track="temporary">$(document).ready(function(){var searchCheckbox=$('div#territory-swap-modal input#territory-swap-show-all-checkbox');function showNoResults(){if($('div#territory-swap-modal ul#country-list-ul li.country:not(.hidden):not(.important-hidden)').length){$('div#territory-swap-modal div#country-list-no-results').addClass('hidden');return;}$('div#territory-swap-modal div#country-list-no-results').removeClass('hidden');}function createSearch(){searchCheckbox.prop('checked',false).trigger("change");var searchInput=$('div#territory-swap-modal input#country-list-search').val().toLowerCase();if(searchInput===''){$('div#territory-swap-modal div.country-list-container li.country.hidden').removeClass('hidden');}else{var searchValues=searchInput.split(' & ');var hiddenString='';$.each(searchValues,function(index,value){$('div#territory-swap-modal div.country-list-container li.country[data-iso*="'+value+'"], div#territory-swap-modal div.country-list-container li.country[data-name*="'+value+'"]').removeClass('hidden');hiddenString+=':not([data-iso*="'+value+'"]):not([data-name*="'+value+'"])';});$('div#territory-swap-modal div.country-list-container li.country'+hiddenString).addClass('hidden');}showNoResults();}var search=$('div#territory-swap-modal input#country-list-search');search.on('keyup',function(){createSearch();});searchCheckbox.change(function(){if($(this).is(':checked')){$('div#territory-swap-modal ul#country-list-ul>li.country').addClass('important-hidden');}else{$('div#territory-swap-modal ul#country-list-ul>li.country').removeClass('important-hidden');}showNoResults();});$('li.see-all a[data-target="#territory-swap-modal"]').on('click',function(){$('div#territory-swap-modal input#country-list-search').val($(this).data('continent'));createSearch();if(!$(this).data('continent')){searchCheckbox.prop('checked',true).trigger("change");}});$('a[data-target="#territory-swap-modal"]:not([data-continent])').on('click',function(){$('div#territory-swap-modal input#country-list-search').val('');createSearch();searchCheckbox.prop('checked',true).trigger("change");});$('div.country-list-container').find('li.country').each(function(){if(this.offsetWidth<this.scrollWidth){$(this).parent().tooltip({title:$(this).text()});}})})</script>
